using BioPestControl.DAL.Entities;
using BioPestControl.DAL.Enums;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace BioPestControl.DAL.Context
{
    public class AppDbContext : DbContext
    {
        public AppDbContext(DbContextOptions<AppDbContext> options) : base(options)
        {
        }

        #region DbSets
        public DbSet<Product> Products { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Category> Categories { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ProductChemical> ProductChemicals { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ProductImage> ProductImages { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Discount> Discounts { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ChemicalProfile> ChemicalProfiles { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Warehouse> Warehouses { get; set; }
        public DbSet<WarehouseProduct> WarehouseProducts { get; set; }
        public DbSet<WarehouseLog> WarehouseLogs { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Article> Articles { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ArticleImage> ArticleImages { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Staff> Staffs { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Customer> Customers { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Order> Orders { get; set; }
        public DbSet<OrderDetail> OrderDetails { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Cart> Carts { get; set; }
        public DbSet<CartItem> CartItems { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Feedback> Feedbacks { get; set; }
        public DbSet<FeedbackImage> FeedbackImages { get; set; }
        public DbSet<FeedbackReply> FeedbackReplies { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ReviewTag> ReviewTags { get; set; }
        public DbSet<FeedbackReviewTag> FeedbackReviewTags { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Crop> Crops { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Pest> Pests { get; set; }
        public DbSet<UsageInstruction> UsageInstructions { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ChemicalConflict> ChemicalConflicts { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Payment> Payments { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Province> Provinces { get; set; }
        public DbSet<District> Districts { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Ward> Wards { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Admin> Admins { get; set; }
        #endregion

        protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
        {
            base.OnModelCreating(modelBuilder);
            //nay cau hinh relationship nghe
            modelBuilder.Entity<Product>()
                .HasOne(p => p.Category)
                .WithMany(c => c.Products)
                .HasForeignKey(p => p.CategoryId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            // Configure optimistic concurrency for Product
            modelBuilder.Entity<Product>()
                .Property(p => p.RowVersion)
                .IsRowVersion();

            modelBuilder.Entity<ProductImage>()
                .HasOne(i => i.Product)
                .WithMany(p => p.ProductImages)
                .HasForeignKey(i => i.ProductId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Staff>(entity =>
            {
                entity.HasIndex(s => s.Email).IsUnique();
            });

            // cau hinh relationship nhieu - nhieu giua Product va ChemicalProfile (h a ch?t  yyyy)

            modelBuilder.Entity<ProductChemical>()
        .HasKey(pc => new { pc.ProductId, pc.ChemicalProfileId });

            // Warehouse / inventory
            modelBuilder.Entity<WarehouseProduct>()
                .HasKey(wp => new { wp.WarehouseId, wp.ProductId });

            modelBuilder.Entity<WarehouseProduct>()
                .HasOne(wp => wp.Warehouse)
                .WithMany(w => w.WarehouseProducts)
                .HasForeignKey(wp => wp.WarehouseId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            modelBuilder.Entity<WarehouseProduct>()
                .HasOne(wp => wp.Product)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(wp => wp.ProductId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            modelBuilder.Entity<WarehouseLog>()
                .HasOne(l => l.Warehouse)
                .WithMany(w => w.WarehouseLogs)
                .HasForeignKey(l => l.WarehouseId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            modelBuilder.Entity<WarehouseLog>()
                .HasOne(l => l.Product)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(l => l.ProductId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            modelBuilder.Entity<ProductChemical>()
                .HasOne(pc => pc.Product)
                .WithMany(p => p.ProductChemicals)
                .HasForeignKey(pc => pc.ProductId);

            modelBuilder.Entity<ProductChemical>()
                .HasOne(pc => pc.ChemicalProfile)
                .WithMany(c => c.ProductChemicals)
                .HasForeignKey(pc => pc.ChemicalProfileId);

            //nay data mau khi tao database
            modelBuilder.Entity<Category>().HasData(
                new Category { Id = 1, Name = "Phân bón và hóa chất" },
                new Category { Id = 2, Name = "Thuốc trừ bệnh" },
                new Category { Id = 3, Name = "Thuốc trừ cỏ" },
                new Category { Id = 4, Name = "Thuốc trừ sâu" }
            );

                    UsageTarget = "Lúa, Rau màu",
                    UsageInstructions = @"### Hướng dẫn sử dụng
                    * Phun khi sâu tuổi 1, 2.
                    * Phun ướt đều toàn cây.
                    * **Lượng nước phun:** 400 – 500 lít/ha.
                    ---
                    ### Hướng dẫn an toàn
                    * Mang đầy đủ đồ bảo hộ lao động.
                    * Không ăn uống, hút thuốc khi pha và phun thuốc.
                    * Không súc rửa dụng cụ phun xịt nơi ao hồ nuôi cá, nguồn nước sinh hoạt.
                    * Để thuốc nơi an toàn, tránh xa tầm tay trẻ em.
                    * Bỏ bao, gói, chai sau khi sử dụng hoặc tiêu huỷ đúng quy định.

            //n y discount
            modelBuilder.Entity<Discount>()
                .HasOne(d => d.Product)
                .WithMany(p => p.Discounts)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.ProductId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            //n y b i vi?t 
            modelBuilder.Entity<ArticleImage>()
                .HasOne(i => i.Article)
                .WithMany(a => a.ArticleImages)
                .HasForeignKey(i => i.ArticleId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);


            //customer and district
            modelBuilder.Entity<Customer>()
                .HasOne(c => c.District)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(c => c.DistrictId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            //order aayyyy
            modelBuilder.Entity<OrderDetail>()
                .HasKey(od => new { od.OrderId, od.ProductId });

            modelBuilder.Entity<OrderDetail>()
                .HasOne(od => od.Order)
                .WithMany(o => o.OrderDetails)
                .HasForeignKey(od => od.OrderId);

            modelBuilder.Entity<OrderDetail>()
                .HasOne(od => od.Product)
                .WithMany(p => p.OrderDetails)
                .HasForeignKey(od => od.ProductId);


            //gi? h ng  yyyyyyyyyyy 
            modelBuilder.Entity<Cart>()
                .HasOne(c => c.Customer)
                .WithOne()
                .HasForeignKey<Cart>(c => c.CustomerId);

            modelBuilder.Entity<CartItem>()
                .HasOne(ci => ci.Cart)
                .WithMany(c => c.Items)
                .HasForeignKey(ci => ci.CartId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);


            modelBuilder.Entity<FeedbackReviewTag>().HasKey(ft => new { ft.FeedbackId, ft.ReviewTagId });

            // Feedback relationships - Prevent multiple cascade paths
            modelBuilder.Entity<Feedback>()
                .HasOne(f => f.Order)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(f => f.OrderId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.NoAction);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Feedback>()
                .HasOne(f => f.Customer)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(f => f.CustomerId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.NoAction);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Feedback>()
                .HasOne(f => f.Product)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(f => f.ProductId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            modelBuilder.Entity<FeedbackImage>()
                .HasOne(fi => fi.Feedback)
                .WithMany(f => f.Images)
                .HasForeignKey(fi => fi.FeedbackId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            modelBuilder.Entity<FeedbackReviewTag>()
                .HasOne(frt => frt.Feedback)
                .WithMany(f => f.FeedbackTags)
                .HasForeignKey(frt => frt.FeedbackId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            modelBuilder.Entity<ChemicalConflict>()
                .HasIndex(c => new { c.ChemicalAId, c.ChemicalBId }).IsUnique();

            // Configure Administrative Units relationships
            modelBuilder.Entity<District>()
                .HasOne(d => d.Province)
                .WithMany(p => p.Districts)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.ProvinceId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Ward>()
                .HasOne(w => w.District)
                .WithMany(d => d.Wards)
                .HasForeignKey(w => w.DistrictId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            // Configure ChemicalConflict relationships properly
            modelBuilder.Entity<ChemicalConflict>()
                .HasOne(c => c.ChemicalA)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(c => c.ChemicalAId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            modelBuilder.Entity<ChemicalConflict>()
                .HasOne(c => c.ChemicalB)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(c => c.ChemicalBId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            modelBuilder.Entity<UsageInstruction>()
                .Property(u => u.AmountPerHectare)
                .HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");

            modelBuilder.Entity<UsageInstruction>()
                .Property(u => u.DosageAmount)
                .HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");

            modelBuilder.Entity<UsageInstruction>()
                .Property(u => u.WaterLiters)
                .HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");

            // Configure Crop and Pest relationships with UsageInstruction
            modelBuilder.Entity<UsageInstruction>()
                .HasOne(u => u.Crop)
                .WithMany(c => c.UsageInstructions)
                .HasForeignKey(u => u.CropId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            modelBuilder.Entity<UsageInstruction>()
                .HasOne(u => u.Pest)
                .WithMany(p => p.UsageInstructions)
                .HasForeignKey(u => u.PestId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            // Configure FeedbackReply relationships
            modelBuilder.Entity<FeedbackReply>()
                .HasOne(fr => fr.Feedback)
                .WithMany(f => f.Replies)
                .HasForeignKey(fr => fr.FeedbackId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            modelBuilder.Entity<FeedbackReply>()
                .HasOne(fr => fr.Staff)
                .WithMany(s => s.FeedbackReplies)
                .HasForeignKey(fr => fr.StaffId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.NoAction);

            // Configure Payment relationships
            modelBuilder.Entity<Payment>()
                .HasOne(p => p.Order)
                .WithMany(o => o.Payments)
                .HasForeignKey(p => p.OrderId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            // Add unique constraint for Payment TransactionId
            modelBuilder.Entity<Payment>()
                .HasIndex(p => p.TransactionId)
                .IsUnique();

            modelBuilder.Entity<Product>()
                .Property(p => p.Price)
                .HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");

            modelBuilder.Entity<ReviewTag>().HasData(
                new ReviewTag { Id = 1, Label = "Fast delivery" },
                new ReviewTag { Id = 2, Label = "Carefully packaged" },

                new ReviewTag { Id = 4, Label = "Good results" },
                new ReviewTag { Id = 5, Label = "Enthusiastic advice" }
                );

            // Configure Admin relationships
            modelBuilder.Entity<Staff>()
                .HasOne(s => s.CreatedByAdmin)
                .WithMany(a => a.ManagedStaffs)
                .HasForeignKey(s => s.CreatedByAdminId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Product>()
                .HasOne(p => p.CreatedByAdmin)
                .WithMany(a => a.ManagedProducts)
                .HasForeignKey(p => p.CreatedByAdminId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Product>()
                .HasOne(p => p.ManagedByStaff)
                .WithMany(s => s.ManagedProducts)
                .HasForeignKey(p => p.ManagedByStaffId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Discount>()
                .HasOne(d => d.CreatedByAdmin)
                .WithMany(a => a.ManagedDiscounts)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.CreatedByAdminId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            modelBuilder.Entity<ChemicalProfile>()
                .HasOne(cp => cp.CreatedByAdmin)
                .WithMany(a => a.ManagedChemicalProfiles)

                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            modelBuilder.Entity<ChemicalProfile>()
                .HasOne(cp => cp.ManagedByStaff)

                .HasForeignKey(cp => cp.ManagedByStaffId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Warehouse>()
                .HasOne(w => w.CreatedByAdmin)
                .WithMany(a => a.ManagedWarehouses)
                .HasForeignKey(w => w.CreatedByAdminId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Warehouse>()
                .HasOne(w => w.ManagedByStaff)
                .WithMany(s => s.ManagedWarehouses)
                .HasForeignKey(w => w.ManagedByStaffId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            modelBuilder.Entity<WarehouseLog>()
                .HasOne(wl => wl.Staff)
                .WithMany(s => s.WarehouseLogs)
                .HasForeignKey(wl => wl.StaffId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            modelBuilder.Entity<WarehouseLog>()
                .HasOne(wl => wl.Admin)
                .WithMany(a => a.WarehouseLogs)
                .HasForeignKey(wl => wl.AdminId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Article>()
                .HasOne(a => a.CreatedByAdmin)
                .WithMany(admin => admin.ManagedArticles)
                .HasForeignKey(a => a.CreatedByAdminId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Article>()
                .HasOne(a => a.ManagedByStaff)
                .WithMany(s => s.ManagedArticles)
                .HasForeignKey(a => a.ManagedByStaffId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            // Configure Staff relationships with Customer and Order
            modelBuilder.Entity<Customer>()
                .HasOne(c => c.ManagedByStaff)
                .WithMany(s => s.ManagedCustomers)
                .HasForeignKey(c => c.ManagedByStaffId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Order>()
                .HasOne(o => o.ManagedByStaff)
                .WithMany(s => s.ManagedOrders)
                .HasForeignKey(o => o.ManagedByStaffId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            // Configure Customer navigation properties
            modelBuilder.Entity<Customer>()
                .HasMany(c => c.Orders)
                .WithOne(o => o.Customer)
                .HasForeignKey(o => o.CustomerId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Customer>()
                .HasMany(c => c.Feedbacks)
                .WithOne(f => f.Customer)
                .HasForeignKey(f => f.CustomerId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.NoAction);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Customer>()
                .HasMany(c => c.Carts)
                .WithOne(cart => cart.Customer)
                .HasForeignKey(cart => cart.CustomerId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            // Configure Product navigation properties
            modelBuilder.Entity<Product>()
                .HasMany(p => p.Feedbacks)
                .WithOne(f => f.Product)
                .HasForeignKey(f => f.ProductId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            modelBuilder.Entity<Product>()
                .HasMany(p => p.WarehouseLogs)
                .WithOne(wl => wl.Product)
                .HasForeignKey(wl => wl.ProductId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            // Configure Order navigation properties
            modelBuilder.Entity<Order>()
                .HasMany(o => o.Feedbacks)
                .WithOne(f => f.Order)
                .HasForeignKey(f => f.OrderId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.NoAction);

            // Seed Admin and Staff
            modelBuilder.Entity<Admin>().HasData(
                new Admin
                {
                    Id = 1,
                    Email = "admin@gmail.com",
                    PasswordHash = BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.HashPassword("123456"),
                    FirstName = "System",
                    LastName = "Admin",
                    Phone = "0123456789",
                    CreatedAt = new DateTime(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc),
                    IsDeleted = false
                }
            );

            modelBuilder.Entity<Staff>().HasData(
                new Staff
                {
                    Id = 1,
                    Email = "staff@gmail.com",
                    PasswordHash = BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.HashPassword("123456"),
                    FirstName = "Sample",
                    LastName = "Staff",
                    Phone = "0987654321",
                    Role = "Staff",
                    CreatedAt = new DateTime(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc),
                    IsDeleted = false
                }
            );
        }

        public async Task SeedAgriculturalDataAsync()
        {
            string Key(string value) => value.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();

            // Normalize existing administrative units in case old runs created duplicated rows.
            var allProvincesForCleanup = await Provinces
                .OrderBy(p => p.Id)
                .ToListAsync();

            foreach (var provinceGroup in allProvincesForCleanup.GroupBy(p => Key(p.Name)).Where(g => g.Count() > 1))
            {
                var canonicalProvince = provinceGroup.First();
                var duplicateProvinceIds = provinceGroup.Skip(1).Select(p => p.Id).ToList();

                // Move districts from duplicate provinces to the canonical province.
                var movedDistricts = await Districts
                    .Where(d => duplicateProvinceIds.Contains(d.ProvinceId))
                    .ToListAsync();

                foreach (var district in movedDistricts)
                {
                    district.ProvinceId = canonicalProvince.Id;
                }

                var duplicatedProvinces = await Provinces
                    .Where(p => duplicateProvinceIds.Contains(p.Id))
                    .ToListAsync();

                if (duplicatedProvinces.Any())
                {
                    Provinces.RemoveRange(duplicatedProvinces);
                }
            }

            await SaveChangesAsync();

            // Deduplicate districts by (ProvinceId + Name), while preserving references.
            var allDistrictsForCleanup = await Districts
                .OrderBy(d => d.Id)
                .ToListAsync();

            foreach (var districtGroup in allDistrictsForCleanup
                .GroupBy(d => new { d.ProvinceId, NameKey = Key(d.Name) })
                .Where(g => g.Count() > 1))
            {
                var canonicalDistrict = districtGroup.First();
                var duplicateDistrictIds = districtGroup.Skip(1).Select(d => d.Id).ToList();

                var customersToUpdate = await Customers
                    .Where(c => c.DistrictId.HasValue && duplicateDistrictIds.Contains(c.DistrictId.Value))
                    .ToListAsync();

                foreach (var customer in customersToUpdate)
                {
                    customer.DistrictId = canonicalDistrict.Id;
                    customer.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
                }

                var wardsToUpdate = await Wards
                    .Where(w => duplicateDistrictIds.Contains(w.DistrictId))
                    .ToListAsync();

                foreach (var ward in wardsToUpdate)
                {
                    ward.DistrictId = canonicalDistrict.Id;
                }

                var duplicatedDistricts = await Districts
                    .Where(d => duplicateDistrictIds.Contains(d.Id))
                    .ToListAsync();

                if (duplicatedDistricts.Any())
                {
                    Districts.RemoveRange(duplicatedDistricts);
                }
            }

            await SaveChangesAsync();

            var desiredProvinces = new List<(string Name, string Code)>
            {
                ("An Giang", "AG"),
                ("Ba Ria - Vung Tau", "BRVT"),
                ("Bac Giang", "BG"),
                ("Bac Kan", "BK"),
                ("Bac Lieu", "BL"),
                ("Bac Ninh", "BN"),
                ("Ben Tre", "BT"),
                ("Binh Dinh", "BDI"),
                ("Binh Duong", "BDU"),
                ("Binh Phuoc", "BPC"),
                ("Binh Thuan", "BTH"),
                ("Ca Mau", "CM"),
                ("Can Tho", "CT"),
                ("Cao Bang", "CB"),
                ("Da Nang", "DN"),
                ("Dak Lak", "DLK"),
                ("Dak Nong", "DNO"),
                ("Dien Bien", "DB"),
                ("Dong Nai", "DNA"),
                ("Dong Thap", "DT"),
                ("Gia Lai", "GL"),
                ("Ha Giang", "HGI"),
                ("Ha Nam", "HNA"),
                ("Ha Noi", "HN"),
                ("Ha Tinh", "HT"),
                ("Hai Duong", "HD"),
                ("Hai Phong", "HP"),
                ("Hau Giang", "HUG"),
                ("Hoa Binh", "HB"),
                ("Hung Yen", "HY"),
                ("Khanh Hoa", "KH"),
                ("Kien Giang", "KG"),
                ("Kon Tum", "KT"),
                ("Lai Chau", "LC"),
                ("Lam Dong", "LD"),
                ("Lang Son", "LS"),
                ("Lao Cai", "LCA"),
                ("Long An", "LA"),
                ("Nam Dinh", "ND"),
                ("Nghe An", "NA"),
                ("Ninh Binh", "NB"),
                ("Ninh Thuan", "NT"),
                ("Phu Tho", "PT"),
                ("Phu Yen", "PY"),
                ("Quang Binh", "QB"),
                ("Quang Nam", "QNA"),
                ("Quang Ngai", "QNG"),
                ("Quang Ninh", "QNH"),
                ("Quang Tri", "QT"),
                ("Soc Trang", "ST"),
                ("Son La", "SL"),
                ("Tay Ninh", "TN"),
                ("Thai Binh", "TB"),
                ("Thai Nguyen", "TNG"),
                ("Thanh Hoa", "TH"),
                ("Thua Thien Hue", "TTH"),
                ("Tien Giang", "TG"),
                ("TP Ho Chi Minh", "HCM"),
                ("Tra Vinh", "TV"),
                ("Tuyen Quang", "TQ"),
                ("Vinh Long", "VL"),
                ("Vinh Phuc", "VP"),
                ("Yen Bai", "YB")
            };

            var existingProvinceKeys = await Provinces
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Select(p => p.Name.Trim().ToLower())
                .ToListAsync();

            var provincesToAdd = desiredProvinces
                .Where(p => !existingProvinceKeys.Contains(Key(p.Name)))
                .Select(p => new Province
                {
                    Name = p.Name,
                    Code = p.Code
                })
                .ToList();

            if (provincesToAdd.Any())
            {
                await Provinces.AddRangeAsync(provincesToAdd);
                await SaveChangesAsync();
            }

            var provinceRows = await Provinces
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Select(p => new { p.Id, NameKey = p.Name.Trim().ToLower() })
                .OrderBy(p => p.Id)
                .ToListAsync();

            var provinceMap = new Dictionary<string, int>();
            foreach (var row in provinceRows)
            {
                // Keep the first province id when duplicated names already exist in DB.
                if (!provinceMap.ContainsKey(row.NameKey))
                {
                    provinceMap[row.NameKey] = row.Id;
                }
            }

            var desiredDistricts = new List<(string ProvinceName, string DistrictName, double Latitude, double Longitude)>
            {
                ("An Giang", "Long Xuyen", 10.3864, 105.4352),
                ("Ba Ria - Vung Tau", "Vung Tau", 10.3459, 107.0843),
                ("Bac Giang", "Bac Giang", 21.2731, 106.1946),
                ("Bac Kan", "Bac Kan", 22.1470, 105.8348),
                ("Bac Lieu", "Bac Lieu", 9.2941, 105.7278),
                ("Bac Ninh", "Bac Ninh", 21.1861, 106.0763),
                ("Ben Tre", "Ben Tre", 10.2434, 106.3759),
                ("Binh Dinh", "Quy Nhon", 13.7829, 109.2197),
                ("Binh Duong", "Thu Dau Mot", 10.9804, 106.6519),
                ("Binh Phuoc", "Dong Xoai", 11.5349, 106.8845),
                ("Binh Thuan", "Phan Thiet", 10.9289, 108.1021),
                ("Ca Mau", "Ca Mau", 9.1769, 105.1524),
                ("Can Tho", "Ninh Kieu", 10.0342, 105.7872),
                ("Cao Bang", "Cao Bang", 22.6666, 106.2636),
                ("Da Nang", "Hai Chau", 16.0544, 108.2022),
                ("Dak Lak", "Buon Ma Thuot", 12.6667, 108.0500),
                ("Dak Nong", "Gia Nghia", 12.0042, 107.6874),
                ("Dien Bien", "Dien Bien Phu", 21.3860, 103.0230),
                ("Dong Nai", "Bien Hoa", 10.9574, 106.8426),
                ("Dong Thap", "Cao Lanh", 10.4600, 105.6329),
                ("Gia Lai", "Pleiku", 13.9833, 108.0000),
                ("Ha Giang", "Ha Giang", 22.8233, 104.9836),
                ("Ha Nam", "Phu Ly", 20.5453, 105.9122),
                ("Ha Noi", "Cau Giay", 21.0302, 105.7829),
                ("Ha Tinh", "Ha Tinh", 18.3428, 105.9057),
                ("Hai Duong", "Hai Duong", 20.9388, 106.3200),
                ("Hai Phong", "Hong Bang", 20.8648, 106.6834),
                ("Hau Giang", "Vi Thanh", 9.7845, 105.4701),
                ("Hoa Binh", "Hoa Binh", 20.8172, 105.3376),
                ("Hung Yen", "Hung Yen", 20.6464, 106.0511),
                ("Khanh Hoa", "Nha Trang", 12.2388, 109.1967),
                ("Kien Giang", "Rach Gia", 10.0125, 105.0809),
                ("Kon Tum", "Kon Tum", 14.3545, 108.0076),
                ("Lai Chau", "Lai Chau", 22.3964, 103.4582),
                ("Lam Dong", "Da Lat", 11.9404, 108.4583),
                ("Lang Son", "Lang Son", 21.8527, 106.7610),
                ("Lao Cai", "Lao Cai", 22.4856, 103.9707),
                ("Long An", "Tan An", 10.5388, 106.4120),
                ("Nam Dinh", "Nam Dinh", 20.4388, 106.1621),
                ("Nghe An", "Vinh", 18.6796, 105.6813),
                ("Ninh Binh", "Ninh Binh", 20.2506, 105.9744),
                ("Ninh Thuan", "Phan Rang - Thap Cham", 11.5650, 108.9886),
                ("Phu Tho", "Viet Tri", 21.3225, 105.4028),
                ("Phu Yen", "Tuy Hoa", 13.0955, 109.3209),
                ("Quang Binh", "Dong Hoi", 17.4689, 106.6223),
                ("Quang Nam", "Tam Ky", 15.5736, 108.4740),
                ("Quang Ngai", "Quang Ngai", 15.1214, 108.8044),
                ("Quang Ninh", "Ha Long", 20.9714, 107.0448),
                ("Quang Tri", "Dong Ha", 16.8163, 107.1003),
                ("Soc Trang", "Soc Trang", 9.6037, 105.9800),
                ("Son La", "Son La", 21.3256, 103.9188),
                ("Tay Ninh", "Tay Ninh", 11.3352, 106.1099),
                ("Thai Binh", "Thai Binh", 20.4463, 106.3365),
                ("Thai Nguyen", "Thai Nguyen", 21.5942, 105.8482),
                ("Thanh Hoa", "Thanh Hoa", 19.8067, 105.7852),
                ("Thua Thien Hue", "Hue", 16.4637, 107.5909),
                ("Tien Giang", "My Tho", 10.3600, 106.3597),
                ("TP Ho Chi Minh", "Quan 1", 10.7769, 106.7009),
                ("Tra Vinh", "Tra Vinh", 9.9347, 106.3453),
                ("Tuyen Quang", "Tuyen Quang", 21.8236, 105.2142),
                ("Vinh Long", "Vinh Long", 10.2537, 105.9722),
                ("Vinh Phuc", "Vinh Yen", 21.3089, 105.6049),
                ("Yen Bai", "Yen Bai", 21.7056, 104.8664)
            };

            var existingDistrictKeys = await Districts
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Select(d => new { d.ProvinceId, NameKey = d.Name.Trim().ToLower() })
                .ToListAsync();

            var districtsToAdd = new List<District>();
            foreach (var item in desiredDistricts)
            {
                if (!provinceMap.TryGetValue(Key(item.ProvinceName), out var provinceId))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                var districtExists = existingDistrictKeys.Any(d =>
                    d.ProvinceId == provinceId && d.NameKey == Key(item.DistrictName));

                if (districtExists)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                districtsToAdd.Add(new District
                {
                    Name = item.DistrictName,
                    ProvinceId = provinceId,
                    Latitude = item.Latitude,
                    Longitude = item.Longitude
                });
            }

            if (districtsToAdd.Any())
            {
                await Districts.AddRangeAsync(districtsToAdd);
                await SaveChangesAsync();
            }

            // Seed crops if empty
            if (!await Crops.AnyAsync())
            {
                await Crops.AddRangeAsync(
                    new Crop { Name = "Lua", Description = "Cay lua" },
                    new Crop { Name = "Ca chua", Description = "Cay ca chua" },
                    new Crop { Name = "Rau cai", Description = "Nhom rau an la" },
                    new Crop { Name = "Xoai", Description = "Cay an trai" }
                );
                await SaveChangesAsync();
            }

            // Seed pests if empty
            if (!await Pests.AnyAsync())
            {
                await Pests.AddRangeAsync(
                    new Pest { Name = "Ray nau" },
                    new Pest { Name = "Sau duc qua" },
                    new Pest { Name = "Sau to" },
                    new Pest { Name = "Rep sap" }
                );
                await SaveChangesAsync();
            }

            // Seed chemical conflicts if missing
            var desiredConflicts = new List<(int A, int B, string Type, string Details)>
            {
                (1, 17, "Precipitation risk", "Boron and Copper can precipitate, reducing effectiveness."),
                (6, 17, "Phytotoxicity risk", "Organophosphate with Copper can increase leaf burn risk on sensitive crops."),
                (5, 8, "Toxicity stacking", "Both are highly toxic; mixing increases operator and crop safety risks."),
                (19, 2, "pH instability", "Mix can destabilize in alkaline conditions and reduce field performance.")
            };

            foreach (var conflict in desiredConflicts)
            {
                // Keep canonical order (A < B) so uniqueness works consistently
                var chemicalAId = Math.Min(conflict.A, conflict.B);
                var chemicalBId = Math.Max(conflict.A, conflict.B);

                var hasChemA = await ChemicalProfiles.AnyAsync(c => c.Id == chemicalAId);
                var hasChemB = await ChemicalProfiles.AnyAsync(c => c.Id == chemicalBId);
                if (!hasChemA || !hasChemB)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                var exists = await ChemicalConflicts.AnyAsync(c =>
                    (c.ChemicalAId == chemicalAId && c.ChemicalBId == chemicalBId) ||
                    (c.ChemicalAId == chemicalBId && c.ChemicalBId == chemicalAId));

                if (!exists)
                {
                    await ChemicalConflicts.AddAsync(new ChemicalConflict
                    {
                        ChemicalAId = chemicalAId,
                        ChemicalBId = chemicalBId,
                        ConflictType = conflict.Type,
                        Details = conflict.Details
                    });
                }
            }

            await SaveChangesAsync();

            // Seed usage instructions for dosage calculation (only when combo does not exist)
            var cropsByName = (await Crops.ToListAsync())
                .GroupBy(c => c.Name)
                .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.First().Id);

            var pestsByName = (await Pests.ToListAsync())
                .GroupBy(p => p.Name)
                .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.First().Id);

            var desiredInstructions = new List<(int ProductId, string Crop, string Pest, decimal Dosage, string Unit, decimal WaterLiters, decimal? PerHa)>
            {
                (4, "Ca chua", "Sau duc qua", 8m, "g", 25m, 150m),
                (6, "Lua", "Ray nau", 40m, "ml", 25m, 1800m),
                (7, "Lua", "Ray nau", 50m, "ml", 25m, 2000m),
                (2, "Rau cai", "Sau to", 100m, "ml", 25m, null),
                (5, "Xoai", "Rep sap", 45m, "ml", 25m, null)
            };

            foreach (var item in desiredInstructions)
            {
                if (!cropsByName.TryGetValue(item.Crop, out var cropId))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (!pestsByName.TryGetValue(item.Pest, out var pestId))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                var hasProduct = await Products.AnyAsync(p => p.Id == item.ProductId);
                if (!hasProduct)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                var exists = await UsageInstructions.AnyAsync(u =>
                    u.ProductId == item.ProductId &&
                    u.CropId == cropId &&
                    u.PestId == pestId);

                if (!exists)
                {
                    await UsageInstructions.AddAsync(new UsageInstruction
                    {
                        ProductId = item.ProductId,
                        CropId = cropId,
                        PestId = pestId,
                        DosageAmount = item.Dosage,
                        DosageUnit = item.Unit,
                        WaterLiters = item.WaterLiters,
                        AmountPerHectare = item.PerHa
                    });
                }
            }

            await SaveChangesAsync();
        }

    }
}
